### K5. v1.3 pre-publication candidate construction

M9.a2 replaces tag-triggered hosted compilation with one deliberately small
operator flow. The implementation must make the following command surfaces
paste-executable with inline PASS output; exact image digest, output paths, and
workflow input names are frozen when the milestone lands:

1. At one clean pushed SHA, create a new candidate generation for intended
   version `v1.3.0`. The command proves local HEAD equals `origin/main`, records
   the peeled SHA, and creates one GitHub draft release targeted at that SHA.
   The draft/provisional tag is not yet an immutable product release.
2. Run the same thin target builder natively as `macos-arm64` on the current
   Mac and inside the pinned native `linux/amd64` container on
   `lexlapax@serenity`. Each command emits
   only the archive, exact-toolchain row, target-smoke row, and digest.
   The macOS builder also proves both manifest-managed Mach-O payloads have
   loader-relative install IDs; Homebrew install must preserve the exact
   archive hashes of the Exqlite NIF and bundled `libcrypto.3.dylib` through
   its mandatory relocation pass.
3. Run that builder as `linux-arm64` inside the same pinned
   multi-architecture Docker image with `--platform linux/arm64`. Both Linux
   paths preflight native OS/architecture, glibc 2.36, image digest,
   `libcrypto.so.3`/`OpenSSL 3.*`, and source-built Exqlite; copy the read-only
   source tree into container storage;
   build/smoke there; and write only final staged output to the mounted output
   directory. Cross-architecture, Rosetta, and QEMU-emulated output are not
   acceptable substitutes.
4. Compose one complete-generation manifest locally, reject missing/duplicate/
   unexpected target files, and upload all three archives plus evidence to the
   draft without `--clobber`. Record returned release ID, asset IDs, and
   digests. The draft remains unpublished.
5. Dispatch the no-build qualification workflow with exact draft release,
   candidate-generation, SHA, manifest, asset-ID, and digest inputs. GitHub
   downloads by ID, rehashes, runs packaged license/TUI protocol rows on all
   targets, and uploads content-free qualification evidence. The operator
   extracts the bound macOS asset and performs the
   simple attach/status/real-turn/health TUI validation before approval.
   Both qualification jobs use job-local `contents: write` because GitHub
   exposes drafts only to push-capable identities; the helper remains GET-only,
   checkout persists no token, and top-level workflow permissions stay empty.
6. Protected promotion revalidates those bindings, signs deterministic
   checksums through GitHub OIDC, uploads signatures, and publishes the same
   draft. GitHub immutable releases must report enabled before this job may
   publish. Installer/tap and installed-product checks consume the published
   bytes.

Failure is phase-specific:

- Before publication, a source, byte, toolchain, smoke, or qualification
  failure records the failed generation. With explicit operator action, remove
  the complete draft and provisional tag and build a new complete generation
  under the same intended version. Never retain a successful target from the
  failed generation or mutate one asset in place.
- During a qualification retry with unchanged bound bytes, reuse the same draft
  asset IDs/digests and emit a new attempt-qualified evidence container.
- After publication, release/tag/assets are immutable and non-reusable; a
  source or byte correction requires a patch version.

The GitHub workflow owns qualification, approval, signing, and publication,
not compilation. The signature identity describes that protected promotion of
operator-built bytes. M9.a2 removes the old build matrix/reuse branches after a
shadow rehearsal; it does not preserve both paths or introduce a release
service.

The frozen operator commands below run in one clean Bash session. They are
deliberately direct: one target helper, ordinary SSH/Docker transport, GitHub
REST, and no release orchestrator. M9.b runs them only after the source SHA and
`1.3.0` version are final.

```sh
set -Eeuo pipefail
umask 077
export REPO=lexlapax/allbert-assist
export GITHUB_REPOSITORY="$REPO"
export VERSION=v1.3.0
export EXPECTED_VERSION="${VERSION#v}"
export SOURCE_SHA="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
export SOURCE_SHORT_SHA="$(printf '%s' "$SOURCE_SHA" | cut -c1-12)"
export CANDIDATE_GENERATION="v13-$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)-$SOURCE_SHORT_SHA"
export CANDIDATE_ROOT="$(mktemp -d /tmp/allbert-v13-candidate.XXXXXX)"
export CANDIDATE_DIR="$CANDIDATE_ROOT/generation"
mkdir -p "$CANDIDATE_DIR"
test -z "$(git status --porcelain)"
test "$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref --symbolic-full-name '@{upstream}')" = origin/main
test "$SOURCE_SHA" = "$(git rev-parse '@{upstream}')"
test "$SOURCE_SHA" = "$(git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main | awk 'NR == 1 {print $1}')"
test "$EXPECTED_VERSION" = "$(sed -n 's/^[[:space:]]*version: "\([^"]*\)".*/\1/p' mix.exs | head -1)"
test "$(gh api -H 'X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2026-03-10' \
  "repos/$REPO/immutable-releases" --jq .enabled)" = true
echo "PASS: clean pushed $SOURCE_SHA; immutable releases enabled"
```

Create the provisional annotated tag and empty draft. They are candidate state
until publication; do not dispatch promotion yet.

```sh
git tag -a "$VERSION" -m "Allbert $EXPECTED_VERSION"
git push origin "$VERSION"
test "$(git rev-parse "$VERSION^{}")" = "$SOURCE_SHA"
test "$(git ls-remote origin "refs/tags/$VERSION^{}" | awk 'NR == 1 {print $1}')" = "$SOURCE_SHA"
jq -S -n --arg tag "$VERSION" --arg sha "$SOURCE_SHA" \
  '{tag_name: $tag, target_commitish: $sha, name: $tag, draft: true,
    prerelease: false, generate_release_notes: false}' \
  > "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/create-draft.json"
gh api --method POST -H 'X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2026-03-10' \
  "repos/$REPO/releases" --input "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/create-draft.json" \
  > "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/draft.json"
export RELEASE_ID="$(jq -er .id "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/draft.json")"
test "$(jq -r .tag_name "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/draft.json")" = "$VERSION"
test "$(jq -r .target_commitish "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/draft.json")" = "$SOURCE_SHA"
test "$(jq -r .draft "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/draft.json")" = true
test "$(jq -r .immutable "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/draft.json")" = false
echo "PASS: empty draft release $RELEASE_ID bound to $VERSION/$SOURCE_SHA"
```

Build macOS arm64 from a disposable exact-SHA worktree on the current Mac. The
Playwright package is the reviewed repository bridge dependency; Chrome is an
external host runtime and both resolved versions enter the toolchain row.

```sh
export REPO_ROOT="$(pwd -P)"
export MAC_PLAYWRIGHT_NODE_PATH="$REPO_ROOT/plugins/allbert.browser/priv/playwright_bridge/node_modules"
export MAC_BROWSER_BINARY_PATH='/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'
export MAC_WORKTREE_ROOT="$(mktemp -d /tmp/allbert-v13-macos-worktree.XXXXXX)"
export MAC_WORKTREE="$MAC_WORKTREE_ROOT/checkout"
export MAC_OUTPUT="$(mktemp -d /tmp/allbert-v13-macos-output.XXXXXX)"
test -f "$MAC_PLAYWRIGHT_NODE_PATH/playwright/package.json"
test -x "$MAC_BROWSER_BINARY_PATH"
git worktree add --detach "$MAC_WORKTREE" "$SOURCE_SHA"
(
  cd "$MAC_WORKTREE"
  ALLBERT_EXPECTED_SHA="$SOURCE_SHA" \
  ALLBERT_CANDIDATE_GENERATION="$CANDIDATE_GENERATION" \
  ALLBERT_BUILDER_CLASS=operator-macos \
  PLAYWRIGHT_NODE_PATH="$MAC_PLAYWRIGHT_NODE_PATH" \
  BROWSER_BINARY_PATH="$MAC_BROWSER_BINARY_PATH" \
    bash scripts/release/build_candidate.sh macos-arm64 "$MAC_OUTPUT"
)
cp "$MAC_OUTPUT"/* "$CANDIDATE_DIR/"
test "$(find "$MAC_OUTPUT" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" -eq 4
echo 'PASS: native macos-arm64 candidate copied'
```

Build Linux x64 on `serenity` in the pinned native amd64 image. The remote
checkout may be refreshed, but it is mounted read-only and cloned without hard
links into disposable container storage. The rolling Arch host therefore
supplies Docker and native x86_64 execution, not the artifact's libc ABI. The
builder also requires Debian `libsctp1 1.0.19+dfsg-2`; the helper copies its
exact shared library into the release, verifies the SONAME/link, and records
the package in toolchain, smoke, and license evidence.

```sh
export SERENITY=lexlapax@serenity
export REMOTE_OUTPUT="/tmp/allbert-v13-$SOURCE_SHORT_SHA-linux-x64"
ssh "$SERENITY" bash -s -- \
  "$SOURCE_SHA" "$CANDIDATE_GENERATION" "$REMOTE_OUTPUT" <<'REMOTE_BUILD'
set -Eeuo pipefail
SOURCE_SHA="$1"; GENERATION="$2"; OUTPUT="$3"
REPO="$HOME/projects/lexlapax/allbert-assist"
IMAGE=hexpm/erlang
IMAGE_DIGEST=sha256:d8c7836b5b2b3b90918fb504b9eac563814503957875658528d9ab4581bf1e6b
NODE_VERSION=22.23.2
NODE_SHA256=d60acfe00a2932254bb0ad20e01b0d74397a0875595de719654b214f4b03f307
ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256=ca481510feb6dabc875bba43e44b25c7abafa53bd7a103639851b7aeace8a022
cd "$REPO"
git fetch origin main
test "$SOURCE_SHA" = "$(git rev-parse origin/main)"
mkdir "$OUTPUT"
docker pull --platform linux/amd64 "$IMAGE@$IMAGE_DIGEST" >/dev/null
test "$(docker image inspect "$IMAGE@$IMAGE_DIGEST" --format '{{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}')" = linux/amd64
docker run --rm --platform linux/amd64 \
  -e SOURCE_SHA="$SOURCE_SHA" -e GENERATION="$GENERATION" \
  -e IMAGE="$IMAGE" -e IMAGE_DIGEST="$IMAGE_DIGEST" \
  -e NODE_VERSION="$NODE_VERSION" -e NODE_SHA256="$NODE_SHA256" \
  -e ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256="$ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256" \
  --mount "type=bind,source=$REPO,target=/input/allbert-assist,readonly" \
  --mount "type=bind,source=$OUTPUT,target=/output" \
  "$IMAGE@$IMAGE_DIGEST" bash -lc '
set -Eeuo pipefail
export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive LC_ALL=C.UTF-8
test "$(uname -s)" = Linux
test "$(uname -m)" = x86_64
test "$(getconf GNU_LIBC_VERSION)" = "glibc 2.36"
apt-get update >/dev/null
apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends build-essential ca-certificates \
  curl git unzip xz-utils jq chromium libcap-dev >/dev/null
mkdir -p /opt/node /opt/elixir-1.19.5 /opt/playwright /work/home /work/source
curl -fsSL -o /tmp/node.tar.xz \
  "https://nodejs.org/dist/v${NODE_VERSION}/node-v${NODE_VERSION}-linux-x64.tar.xz"
printf "%s  %s\n" "$NODE_SHA256" /tmp/node.tar.xz | sha256sum -c -
tar -xJf /tmp/node.tar.xz --strip-components=1 -C /opt/node
curl -fsSL -o /tmp/elixir.zip \
  https://github.com/elixir-lang/elixir/releases/download/v1.19.5/elixir-otp-28.zip
printf "%s  %s\n" "$ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256" /tmp/elixir.zip | sha256sum -c -
unzip -q /tmp/elixir.zip -d /opt/elixir-1.19.5
PATH=/opt/node/bin:$PATH PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1 \
  /opt/node/bin/npm install --prefix /opt/playwright --ignore-scripts \
    --no-audit --no-fund playwright@1.58.2 >/dev/null
git config --global --add safe.directory /input/allbert-assist/.git
git clone --no-hardlinks --no-checkout /input/allbert-assist /work/source >/dev/null
git -C /work/source checkout --detach "$SOURCE_SHA" >/dev/null
test -z "$(git -C /work/source status --porcelain)"
useradd --create-home --uid 1000 --shell /bin/bash allbert-builder
chown -R allbert-builder:allbert-builder /work/home /work/source /output
runuser -u allbert-builder -- env LC_ALL=C.UTF-8 \
  PATH=/opt/elixir-1.19.5/bin:/opt/node/bin:/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin \
  HOME=/work/home MIX_HOME=/work/home/.mix HEX_HOME=/work/home/.hex \
  bash -c "set -Eeuo pipefail; mix local.hex 2.5.1 --force; \
    mix local.rebar --force; cd /work/source; \
    ALLBERT_EXPECTED_SHA=$SOURCE_SHA \
    ALLBERT_CANDIDATE_GENERATION=$GENERATION \
    ALLBERT_BUILDER_CLASS=docker-linux \
    ALLBERT_CONTAINER_IMAGE=$IMAGE \
    ALLBERT_CONTAINER_IMAGE_DIGEST=$IMAGE_DIGEST \
    PLAYWRIGHT_NODE_PATH=/opt/playwright/node_modules \
    BROWSER_BINARY_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium \
      bash scripts/release/build_candidate.sh linux-x64 /output"
'
test "$(find "$OUTPUT" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" -eq 4
REMOTE_BUILD
scp "$SERENITY:$REMOTE_OUTPUT/allbert-v$EXPECTED_VERSION-linux-x64.tar.gz" \
  "$SERENITY:$REMOTE_OUTPUT/allbert-v$EXPECTED_VERSION-linux-x64.tar.gz.sha256" \
  "$SERENITY:$REMOTE_OUTPUT/toolchain-linux-x64.json" \
  "$SERENITY:$REMOTE_OUTPUT/smoke-linux-x64.json" "$CANDIDATE_DIR/"
echo 'PASS: pinned glibc-2.36/OpenSSL-3 linux-x64 candidate with artifact-owned libsctp copied'
```

Build Linux arm64 from the same exact multi-architecture manifest on Apple
Silicon. A disposable Docker CLI config avoids desktop credential-helper coupling while
copying only the active context's non-secret metadata needed for Docker Desktop
connectivity. Image/tool installation runs as container root. The read-only
exact-SHA repository is cloned without hard links into container storage, and
the entire candidate build and smoke run there as the ephemeral unprivileged
user.

```sh
export ARM_IMAGE=hexpm/erlang
export ARM_IMAGE_DIGEST=sha256:d8c7836b5b2b3b90918fb504b9eac563814503957875658528d9ab4581bf1e6b
export NODE_VERSION=22.23.2
export NODE_ARM64_SHA256=fff4078c5def658577f92c88db7db3bc0072924bfb93fe52c1e744a54e94abb8
export ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256=ca481510feb6dabc875bba43e44b25c7abafa53bd7a103639851b7aeace8a022
export ARM_OUTPUT="$(mktemp -d /tmp/allbert-v13-linux-arm64.XXXXXX)"
export CONTAINER_SOURCE=/input/allbert-assist
export DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG="$(mktemp -d /tmp/allbert-docker-config.XXXXXX)"
export ACTIVE_DOCKER_CONTEXT="$(docker context show)"
export DOCKER_CONTEXT_META="$(docker context inspect "$ACTIVE_DOCKER_CONTEXT" \
  --format '{{.Storage.MetadataPath}}')"
test -f "$DOCKER_CONTEXT_META/meta.json"
mkdir -p "$DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG/contexts/meta"
cp -R "$DOCKER_CONTEXT_META" "$DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG/contexts/meta/"
jq -S -n --arg currentContext "$ACTIVE_DOCKER_CONTEXT" \
  '{auths: {}, currentContext: $currentContext}' \
  > "$DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG/config.json"
docker --config "$DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG" pull --platform linux/arm64 \
  "$ARM_IMAGE@$ARM_IMAGE_DIGEST"
test "$(docker --config "$DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG" image inspect \
  "$ARM_IMAGE@$ARM_IMAGE_DIGEST" --format '{{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}')" = linux/arm64
test "$(docker --config "$DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG" image inspect \
  "$ARM_IMAGE@$ARM_IMAGE_DIGEST" --format '{{index .RepoDigests 0}}')" = \
  "hexpm/erlang@$ARM_IMAGE_DIGEST"
docker --config "$DOCKER_PUBLIC_CONFIG" run --rm --platform linux/arm64 \
  -e SOURCE_SHA="$SOURCE_SHA" -e GENERATION="$CANDIDATE_GENERATION" \
  -e IMAGE="$ARM_IMAGE" -e IMAGE_DIGEST="$ARM_IMAGE_DIGEST" \
  -e CONTAINER_SOURCE="$CONTAINER_SOURCE" \
  -e NODE_VERSION="$NODE_VERSION" -e NODE_SHA256="$NODE_ARM64_SHA256" \
  -e ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256="$ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256" \
  --mount "type=bind,source=$REPO_ROOT,target=$CONTAINER_SOURCE,readonly" \
  --mount "type=bind,source=$ARM_OUTPUT,target=/output" \
  "$ARM_IMAGE@$ARM_IMAGE_DIGEST" bash -lc '
set -Eeuo pipefail
export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive LC_ALL=C.UTF-8
test "$(uname -s)" = Linux
test "$(uname -m)" = aarch64
test "$(getconf GNU_LIBC_VERSION)" = "glibc 2.36"
apt-get update >/dev/null
apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends build-essential ca-certificates \
  curl git unzip xz-utils jq chromium libcap-dev >/dev/null
useradd --create-home --uid 1000 --shell /bin/bash allbert-builder
mkdir -p /opt/node /opt/elixir-1.19.5 /opt/playwright /work/home /work/source
curl -fsSL -o /tmp/node.tar.xz \
  "https://nodejs.org/dist/v${NODE_VERSION}/node-v${NODE_VERSION}-linux-arm64.tar.xz"
printf "%s  %s\n" "$NODE_SHA256" /tmp/node.tar.xz | sha256sum -c -
tar -xJf /tmp/node.tar.xz --strip-components=1 -C /opt/node
curl -fsSL -o /tmp/elixir.zip https://github.com/elixir-lang/elixir/releases/download/v1.19.5/elixir-otp-28.zip
printf "%s  %s\n" "$ELIXIR_ZIP_SHA256" /tmp/elixir.zip | sha256sum -c -
unzip -q /tmp/elixir.zip -d /opt/elixir-1.19.5
PATH=/opt/node/bin:$PATH PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BROWSER_DOWNLOAD=1 \
  /opt/node/bin/npm install --prefix /opt/playwright \
  --ignore-scripts --no-audit --no-fund playwright@1.58.2 >/dev/null
git config --global --add safe.directory "$CONTAINER_SOURCE/.git"
test "$(git -C "$CONTAINER_SOURCE" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$SOURCE_SHA"
git clone --no-hardlinks --no-checkout "$CONTAINER_SOURCE" /work/source >/dev/null
git -C /work/source checkout --detach "$SOURCE_SHA" >/dev/null
test "$(git -C /work/source rev-parse HEAD)" = "$SOURCE_SHA"
test -z "$(git -C /work/source status --porcelain)"
chown -R allbert-builder:allbert-builder /work/home /work/source /output
runuser -u allbert-builder -- env LC_ALL=C.UTF-8 \
  PATH=/opt/elixir-1.19.5/bin:/opt/node/bin:/usr/local/sbin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/sbin:/usr/bin:/sbin:/bin \
  HOME=/work/home MIX_HOME=/work/home/.mix HEX_HOME=/work/home/.hex \
  bash -c "set -Eeuo pipefail; \
    mix local.hex 2.5.1 --force; \
    mix local.rebar --force; \
    cd /work/source; \
    ALLBERT_EXPECTED_SHA=$SOURCE_SHA \
    ALLBERT_CANDIDATE_GENERATION=$GENERATION \
    ALLBERT_BUILDER_CLASS=docker-linux \
    ALLBERT_CONTAINER_IMAGE=$IMAGE \
    ALLBERT_CONTAINER_IMAGE_DIGEST=$IMAGE_DIGEST \
    PLAYWRIGHT_NODE_PATH=/opt/playwright/node_modules \
    BROWSER_BINARY_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium \
      bash scripts/release/build_candidate.sh linux-arm64 /output"
'
cp "$ARM_OUTPUT"/* "$CANDIDATE_DIR/"
test "$(find "$ARM_OUTPUT" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" -eq 4
test "$(find "$CANDIDATE_DIR" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type f | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" -eq 12
echo 'PASS: pinned glibc-2.36/OpenSSL-3 linux-arm64 candidate with artifact-owned libsctp copied; complete local generation has 12 files'
```

Prove the candidate survives Homebrew's mandatory source-formula Mach-O
relocation. Homebrew 6 rejects an arbitrary formula path, so create one
temporary no-git tap and address its formula by full name. The formula preserves
and restores exactly the two known manifest-managed Mach-O payloads after
Homebrew's unconditional relocation pass; this is a bounded packaging seam,
not a general manifest interpreter. A loopback server supplies the still-
private archive, and the shadow formula declares the version explicitly because
Homebrew otherwise infers one from the loopback URL. This temporarily upgrades
the installed `allbert` formula to the candidate. Retain the temporary tap
through attended validation; if the generation is abandoned, reinstall the
current published tap formula and remove the temporary tap.

```sh
export HOMEBREW_SHADOW_ROOT="$(mktemp -d /tmp/allbert-v13-homebrew.XXXXXX)"
export HOMEBREW_SHADOW_PORT="${HOMEBREW_SHADOW_PORT:-49191}"
export HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP=allbert-v13/candidate
export MAC_ARCHIVE="allbert-v$EXPECTED_VERSION-macos-arm64.tar.gz"
export MAC_ARCHIVE_SHA256="$(shasum -a 256 "$CANDIDATE_DIR/$MAC_ARCHIVE" | awk '{print $1}')"
test -z "$(brew tap | grep -x "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP" || true)"
brew tap-new --no-git "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP"
export HOMEBREW_SHADOW_FORMULA="$(brew --repository "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP")/Formula/allbert.rb"
cp homebrew/allbert.rb "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_FORMULA"
cp "$CANDIDATE_DIR/$MAC_ARCHIVE" "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_ROOT/$MAC_ARCHIVE"
ruby - "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_FORMULA" \
  "http://127.0.0.1:$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_PORT/$MAC_ARCHIVE" \
  "$MAC_ARCHIVE_SHA256" "$EXPECTED_VERSION" <<'RUBY'
path, url, sha, version = ARGV
body = File.read(path)
pattern = /(on_macos do\s+on_arm do\s+url )"[^"]+"(\s+sha256 )"[^"]+"/
abort "macOS formula block not found" unless body.sub!(pattern, "\\1#{url.dump}\\2#{sha.dump}")
license = "  license \"Apache-2.0\"\n"
abort "license anchor not found" unless body.sub!(license, "#{license}  version #{version.dump}\n")
File.write(path, body)
RUBY
test "$(brew info --json=v2 "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP/allbert" \
  | jq -r '.formulae[0].versions.stable')" = "$EXPECTED_VERSION"
python3 -m http.server "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_PORT" --bind 127.0.0.1 \
  --directory "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_ROOT" >"$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_ROOT/http.log" 2>&1 &
HOMEBREW_HTTP_PID=$!
trap 'kill "$HOMEBREW_HTTP_PID" 2>/dev/null || true' EXIT
if brew list --versions allbert >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  HOMEBREW_NO_AUTO_UPDATE=1 brew reinstall --formula "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP/allbert"
else
  HOMEBREW_NO_AUTO_UPDATE=1 brew install --formula "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP/allbert"
fi
HOMEBREW_NO_AUTO_UPDATE=1 brew test "$HOMEBREW_SHADOW_TAP/allbert"
"$(brew --prefix allbert)/bin/allbert" licenses --json >/dev/null
export HOMEBREW_ARCHIVE_ROOT="$(mktemp -d /tmp/allbert-v13-homebrew-archive.XXXXXX)"
tar -xzf "$CANDIDATE_DIR/$MAC_ARCHIVE" -C "$HOMEBREW_ARCHIVE_ROOT"
for HOMEBREW_MANAGED_RELATIVE in \
  lib/crypto-5.9/priv/lib/libcrypto.3.dylib \
  lib/exqlite-0.39.0/priv/sqlite3_nif.so; do
  test "$(shasum -a 256 "$HOMEBREW_ARCHIVE_ROOT/allbert/$HOMEBREW_MANAGED_RELATIVE" | awk '{print $1}')" = \
    "$(shasum -a 256 "$(brew --prefix allbert)/libexec/$HOMEBREW_MANAGED_RELATIVE" | awk '{print $1}')"
done
kill "$HOMEBREW_HTTP_PID"
wait "$HOMEBREW_HTTP_PID" || true
trap - EXIT
echo 'PASS: Homebrew restored the two sealed Mach-O payloads and installed license verification passed'
```

Stage only that complete directory. The helper validates every source SHA,
generation, target, toolchain/runtime row, smoke row, archive digest, and the
exact file set before the first upload. It uploads without clobber and writes
the candidate manifest last.

```sh
export CANDIDATE_MANIFEST="$CANDIDATE_ROOT/candidate-manifest.json"
bash scripts/release/stage_artifacts.sh stage-generation \
  "$RELEASE_ID" "$VERSION" "$SOURCE_SHA" "$CANDIDATE_GENERATION" \
  "$CANDIDATE_DIR" "$CANDIDATE_MANIFEST" \
  | tee "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/stage-result.txt"
export CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_ASSET_ID="$(sed -n 's/^manifest_asset_id=//p' "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/stage-result.txt")"
export CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_ASSET_DIGEST="$(sed -n 's/^manifest_asset_digest=//p' "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/stage-result.txt")"
[[ "$CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_ASSET_ID" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]]
[[ "$CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_ASSET_DIGEST" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{64}$ ]]
test "$(gh api --paginate --slurp \
  "repos/$REPO/releases/$RELEASE_ID/assets?per_page=100" \
  | jq '[.[][]] | length')" -eq 13
echo 'PASS: complete 13-asset draft generation staged'
```

Dispatch and watch no-build qualification. Read back the one attempt-qualified
manifest artifact by ID and API digest; do not infer it from a filename alone.
The two jobs that execute Linux packages run on Ubuntu 24.04, whose glibc is at
or above the artifact's frozen 2.36 floor; evidence composition and promotion
remain on Ubuntu 22.04 because they do not execute the package. `qualify-target`
checks that floor before downloading assets and reports an explicit qualifier-
host ABI error rather than allowing a loader diagnostic to masquerade as JSON.
For both Linux targets it also requires the artifact-owned SCTP row and parses
the unfiltered packaged `licenses --json` stdout as exactly one manifest; a
host-installed `libsctp` is not accepted as artifact closure evidence.

```sh
gh workflow run release-artifacts.yml --ref "$VERSION" \
  -f operation=qualification -f release_id="$RELEASE_ID" -f tag="$VERSION" \
  -f source_sha="$SOURCE_SHA" -f candidate_generation="$CANDIDATE_GENERATION" \
  -f candidate_manifest_asset_id="$CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_ASSET_ID" \
  -f candidate_manifest_asset_digest="$CANDIDATE_MANIFEST_ASSET_DIGEST"
QUALIFICATION_RUN_ID=""
for ((V13_RUN_LOOKUP_ATTEMPT=1; V13_RUN_LOOKUP_ATTEMPT<=30; V13_RUN_LOOKUP_ATTEMPT++)); do
  QUALIFICATION_RUN_ID="$(gh run list --workflow release-artifacts.yml \
    --event workflow_dispatch --branch "$VERSION" --commit "$SOURCE_SHA" --limit 1 \
    --json databaseId --jq '.[0].databaseId // empty')"
  if [[ "$QUALIFICATION_RUN_ID" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]]; then break; fi
  sleep 1
done
[[ "$QUALIFICATION_RUN_ID" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]]
gh run watch "$QUALIFICATION_RUN_ID" --exit-status
gh api --paginate --slurp "repos/$REPO/actions/runs/$QUALIFICATION_RUN_ID/artifacts?per_page=100" \
  | jq -e '[.[].artifacts[] | select(.name | endswith("-manifest"))] | if length == 1 then .[0] else error("expected one manifest") end' \
  > "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/qualification-artifact.json"
export QUALIFICATION_ARTIFACT_ID="$(jq -er .id "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/qualification-artifact.json")"
export QUALIFICATION_ARTIFACT_DIGEST="$(jq -er '.digest | sub("^sha256:"; "")' "$CANDIDATE_ROOT/qualification-artifact.json")"
[[ "$QUALIFICATION_ARTIFACT_ID" =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]]
[[ "$QUALIFICATION_ARTIFACT_DIGEST" =~ ^[0-9a-f]{64}$ ]]
echo 'PASS: exact draft generation qualified without a build job'
```

Execution 2026-08-04: exact candidate generation
`v13-20260804T071638Z-a3cd5781617b` at full SHA
`a3cd5781617b3a9ee511425b687ff359bdfcb0c7` passed all three native builders,
the restrictive-umask Homebrew install/license/mode/Mach-O row, complete
13-asset staging, and no-build qualification run `30888023502`. Draft release
`364678592` remains unpublished and contains candidate-manifest asset
`501038434` (`a8dd0fef2b793fdaf5d769e2bb671330540519bc442886179d47ce0b2e63c54c`);
the content-free qualification manifest is artifact `8883887082`
(`cf2576e5954c98490c0264157b11511984e35b794b87547a540d3f356b765e9a`).
The first frozen artifact-read command incorrectly traversed GitHub's numeric
`total_count` as if it were an artifact. The corrected query above scopes
iteration to each page's `artifacts` array; qualification itself was already
green and was not rerun for this documentation-only command correction.

After K6's four packaged latency cells pass, run deferred PV-0 through PV-6
above against the exact installed candidate, then continue with the short
parity row below. Do not repeat the source proposal, temporal, archive, Forget,

```sh

echo "Exact packaged central source: $V13_PACKAGE_SEARCH_SOURCE_LINE"
```

The v1.3 pre-publication corrective loop is deliberately batched:

1. Classify a finding as `environment/unavailable`, `documentation-only`, or
   `executable/artifact` and stop only the affected FV.
2. Environment setup is corrected without changing product evidence; repeat
   the affected row. A genuinely unconfigured real channel/provider remains
   `UNAVAILABLE`, never a product pass.
3. A documentation-only correction runs `git diff --check`, the docs gate, and
   the affected operator row. It does not rebuild an artifact or repeat either
   aggregate. This narrow inheritance is allowed only when the diff from the
   last aggregate-gated SHA is confined to this active plan/request-flow and
   changes no code, configuration, dependency lock, release script, packaged
   documentation, ADR contract, or generated manifest.
4. Executable/artifact findings are collected into one bounded remediation
   tranche. Each commit gets its focused owner tests and targeted static checks;
   no aggregate runs per commit. At the tranche's final clean pushed SHA,
   replace the complete three-target draft generation, refresh all four
   packaged latency cells, and repeat only affected FV rows. After operator
   acceptance, run structural proof, `release.v13`, and the authoritative
   release gate once in an isolated checkout. An unrelated inherited-lane fix
   repeats that aggregate after the batched correction but does not repeat
   accepted operator rows unless it changes their product bytes or contracts.
5. Before publication, the replacement keeps the intended `1.3.0` version.
   After publication, every executable/artifact correction uses a new patch
   version.

coordinator terminalized the historical children before the test's injected
coordinator started. Add a synchronous Scheduler mailbox barrier after the
pending directives and before acknowledgement, then prove Registry ownership
is still empty. Production Scheduler/Steering/lifecycle behavior must not
change. Run the exact row repeatedly, the complete owner file, and the exact

the final K6 rejoin; PV-0 through PV-8 remain inherited and not relabelled.

Focused reproduction at seed `795785` classified the failures without changing
production code. Jobs repeated `1/4` RED only because the LiveView test expected
the obsolete `mix allbert.confirmations show`; the action response and packaged
CLI contract already agree on `allbert admin confirmations show`. The initial
OpenAI diagnosis was wrong: the roughly 6.2-second observation included the
five-second selector failure and setup, not a healthy composition. A serial
45-second poll/50-second await still returned `:composition_not_queued`.
Comparison with ACP and the M9.b.12.d history identifies the actual cause:
OpenAI's three decomposition rows do not call the shared
`FanoutRoles.configure!/0` admission fixture, so callable-role readiness rejects
fan-out and the request follows the ordinary direct-answer path. No composition
can be queued in that state.

Update the Jobs assertions to the packaged command, put the three OpenAI rows
through the existing `FanoutRoles` seam, and move ACP's already-proven 45-second
poll/50-second await policy into `FanoutReportFixture` for both public-protocol
owners. Delete the duplicate private loops. Run the two web owners and ACP
serially because their Repo/application state is shared. The first admitted
OpenAI run joined all three rows and exposed one additional stale assertion:
the non-streaming row expected model-disabled quality failures even though the
shared seam enables the model and its deterministic runner completes both
children. Assert completed children here; the Worker owner suite retains the
unavailable-quality branch, while this owner proves OpenAI transport parity.
Then repeat the three
OpenAI rows and run compile/format/scoped Credo, docs, and diff checks. Do not

## L. Implementation And Closeout Checklist

**Build Progress, 2026-08-02 — c3baec24 executable/focused/static/audit
rejoin.** The broad focused command ran 365 tests: 363 passed and two stale
test-contract assertions failed. A test-only authority correction changed no
production code; the exact invalidated rows then passed `2/2`, the complete
DirectAnswer owner file passed `35/35`, and the complete Settings-actions owner
file passed `32/32`. The bounded union—not the original 363/365 result by
itself—is the focused evidence. Whole-project test and development warnings-as-
errors compiles, the whole-project formatter, strict Credo over 1,610 files and
30,345 modules/functions with zero issues, cached whole-project Dialyzer with
zero errors/skips/unnecessary skips, `deps.unlock --check-unused`, `mix hex.audit`
with `No retired or security advisory packages found`, scoped git-diff checks,
and the independent executable audit passed. The configured-provider matrix,
agent FOV, operator FOV, full SV, package, and aggregate gates remain pending;
none is claimed by this evidence.

**Build Progress, 2026-08-02 — e805045f default-7B matrix RED.** The exact
clean pushed two-phase gate used the legacy `--profile direct_answer_local`
all-equal shorthand. It wrote one profile to each task route but did not emit
independent four-role binding/configuration attestation. Manager passed `1/2`:
supplied data passed in one attempt, while
the adaptive row returned a reviewed but semantically wrong
`answer/supplied_data` after two physical attempts. Composer was not evaluated;
its seven rows correctly carry `manager_admission/manager_row_failed` and null
phase counts. Worker passed `0/5`, but every row closed its phase, rule-group,
catalog, per-row review/revision configuration, and call evidence through exact
revised `1/2/1/2/6` arithmetic. Across the five rows the gate recorded 30 logical
protocol calls, 20 live critic invocations, five live revisions, five final
`unresolved` verdicts, one of two required changes made, and zero optional
changes. Wall times were 21,532 ms and 105,279 ms. The fixture supplies each
draft, so a revised Worker row has five live provider invocations even though
its production protocol count is six. Independent review classifies this as a
genuine semantic miss, not transport/schema/evidence failure, and preserves
composer-not-evaluated as distinct from composer failure.
